A genesis (3016-3020; header 2327-2339; empty index 2992-2999) has 0 keys and 0 rows. A load goes through `walk_generations` (3201-3239), which keeps the header the census was walked under. `walk` counts keys as distinct index inserts (3311-3312) and rows as the sum over the index (3332-3337), and refuses any header that disagrees (3338-3349). `partitions()` (3536-3559) splits exactly those index values over `SEGMENTS` = [Index, Cash, Fno] (891), which is every `Segment` variant (crates/core/src/instrument.rs:74-81). Each partition is `epoch_of` (3481-3501): `genesis_partition` with 0/0 (2348-2367), then `record_held` of distinct keys, each adding one key and its rows (3969-3976, 4017-4024). Any refusal exits at 2353, before 2356. So the sums always equal `keys()`/`total_rows()` (3415-3423).

Each partition is `epoch_of` (3481-3501): `genesis_partition` with 0/0 (2348-2367), then `record_held` of distinct keys, each adding one key and its rows (3969-3976, 4017-4024). Any refusal exits at 2353, before 2356. So the sums always equal `keys()`/`total_rows()` (3415-3423).
